k^{\star}
rounds of two queries each; each query is one run of
Lemma
S2.43
with
T
gradient steps plus one terminal value
evaluation; each step solves
n
inner problems by
Lemma
S2.42
(i), each to accuracy
\varepsilon_{x}
in

k_{\mathrm{in}}
preconditioned gradient steps, each step one oracle call.
The expressions for
k^{\star},T,k_{\mathrm{in}}
restate the choices in
Lemmas
S2.43
and
S2.42
(the bound on

k_{\mathrm{in}}
uses the initial-gap bound of
Lemma
S2.42
(i) and
(1-m/M)\leq 2/5
). First impose the
additional bounds
\mathsf{s}_{n}\vee\bar{\mathsf{m}}_{n}\leq n^{1/4}
,

\varkappa_{n}\leq\bar{\varkappa}
, and
\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\leq n
. On

\mathfrak{E}_{n}
, every quantity inside the three logarithms is then bounded
above and below by fixed
powers of
n
times fixed constants:
U_{n}\leq\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2}\leq n^{1/2}

and
\delta=\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n\leq 1
give

\Lambda_{\delta}\sqrt{U_{n}/\delta}=3\varkappa_{n}\sqrt{U_{n}\delta}\leq 3\bar{\varkappa}\,n^{1/4}
and

\mathsf{M}_{\delta}+\mathsf{L}_{\delta}\leq Cd_{h}\,n^{1/2}
; the third condition
in (30) and
\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}^{-1}=O(n^{c})
give

\sqrt{\ell_{n}\delta}\geq 8\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}\geq c_{\varepsilon}n^{-c}
,
whence
\mu_{\delta}^{-1}=8\ell_{n}^{-1}\sqrt{U_{n}/\delta}=8\sqrt{\varkappa_{n}}\big/\sqrt{\ell_{n}\delta}\leq C_{\varepsilon}\sqrt{\bar{\varkappa}}\,n^{c}
; and
\varepsilon_{x}^{-1}
is polynomial in

n,\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}^{-1},\mathsf{s}_{n},\mu_{\delta}^{-1}
. Hence

k^{\star},\,T/\varkappa_{n}^{3/2},\,k_{\mathrm{in}}=O((1+c)\log n)
and the
product of the three factors is
O((1+c)^{3}\bar{\varkappa}^{3/2}\,n\log^{3}n)
.

It remains to show that these empirical bounds hold simultaneously with high
probability. Let
\sigma_{\min}\triangleq\lambda_{\min}(V)>0
and define

\mathcal{A}_{n}\triangleq\left\{\ell_{n}\geq\tfrac{\sigma_{\min}}{2}\right\}\cap\left\{\Tr(\mathcal{V}_{n})\leq\Tr(V)+1\right\}\cap\left\{\Tr(\mathcal{W}_{n})\leq\Tr(W)+1\right\}\cap\left\{\lambda_{\min}(\mathcal{W}_{n})\geq\tfrac{\lambda_{\min}(W)}{2}\right\}.

The first and last events have probability
1-O(1/n)
by
Lemma
S2.11
applied to

A=\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)^{\top}
(as in Proposition
S2.12
,
part (A1)) and to
A=\mathbf{h}(X)^{\otimes 2}
(both admissible since
Assumption 2.9 gives finite fourth moments of

\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\right\|_{2}
and
\left\|\mathbf{h}(X)\right\|_{2}
). The two trace events have
probability
1-O(1/n)
by Chebyshev’s inequality applied to the scalar
averages
\Tr(\mathcal{V}_{n})
and
\Tr(\mathcal{W}_{n})
, whose variances are
O(1/n)

under Assumption 2.9. On
\mathcal{A}_{n}
,

U_{n}\leq\Tr(V)+1
and
\varkappa_{n}\leq 2(\Tr(V)+1)/\sigma_{\min}\triangleq\bar{\varkappa}
, so

\delta_{0}\geq\underline{\delta}_{0}\triangleq\frac{\sigma_{\min}/2}{K_{\mathrm{curv}}^{2}(3+2\sqrt{\bar{\varkappa}})^{2}}>0
. Moreover, since

\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}
is the
(1-\varrho)
-quantile of

v^{\top}\mathcal{W}_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{V}_{n}^{-1}\mathcal{W}_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}v
with
v
standard
normal,

\underline{z}\triangleq\frac{\lambda_{\min}(W)\,\chi^{2}_{d_{h};1-\varrho}}{2(\Tr(V)+1)}\leq\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\leq\frac{2(\Tr(W)+1)\,\chi^{2}_{d_{h};1-\varrho}}{\sigma_{\min}}\triangleq\bar{z}\qquad\text{on }\mathcal{A}_{n}.

Define

\mathfrak{C}_{n}\triangleq\mathcal{A}_{n}\cap\left\{\mathsf{s}_{n}\leq n^{1/4}\right\}.

Because
\mathsf{s}_{n}=\max_{i\in[n]}\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\Sigma^{1/2}\right\|_{2}
,
Assumption 2.9 and a union–Markov bound give

\mathbb{P}^{\star}\left(\mathsf{s}_{n}>n^{1/4}\right)\leq n\,\mathbb{P}^{\star}\left(\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\Sigma^{1/2}\right\|_{2}>n^{1/4}\right)\leq\frac{\mathds{E}\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\Sigma^{1/2}\right\|_{2}^{8}}{n}=O(n^{-1}).

Consequently,
\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathfrak{C}_{n}^{c})=O(n^{-1})
. On
\mathfrak{C}_{n}
,

\bar{\mathsf{m}}_{n}^{2}=\Tr(\mathcal{W}_{n})\leq\Tr(W)+1
,

\varkappa_{n}\leq\bar{\varkappa}
, and
\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\leq\bar{z}
.
Thus, for all sufficiently large
n
, the empirical bounds used above hold on

\mathfrak{C}_{n}
, proving the asserted work bound on

\mathfrak{E}_{n}\cap\mathfrak{C}_{n}
.

For the accuracy in part (iv), take
n\geq N_{0}
, where
N_{0}
depends only on

(\sigma_{\min},\Tr V,\Tr W,\lambda_{\min}(W),K_{\mathrm{curv}},\varrho).

On
\mathcal{A}_{n}
,

\delta=\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n\leq\bar{z}/n\leq\underline{\delta}_{0}/2\leq\delta_{0}/2
and

\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}=n^{-3/2}\log^{-2}n\leq\frac{1}{8}\sqrt{\ell_{n}\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n}

(the right side is at least

\frac{1}{8}\sqrt{\sigma_{\min}\underline{z}/2}\,n^{-1/2}
). Hence

\mathcal{A}_{n}\subseteq\mathfrak{E}_{n}
, and therefore

\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathfrak{E}_{n}\cap\mathfrak{C}_{n})=\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathfrak{C}_{n})=1-O(n^{-1})
.

(iv)
Now take
\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}=1/(n^{3/2}\log^{2}n)
. Write

R\triangleq\{nR_{n}(\mathbf{h})>\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\}
and

\hat{R}\triangleq\{\hat{\mathsf{T}}_{n}\text{ rejects}\}
. By part (ii),

\hat{R}\subseteq R
and

R\setminus\hat{R}\subseteq\{0<nR_{n}(\mathbf{h})-\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\leq w_{n}\}\cup\mathfrak{E}_{n}^{c}
with

w_{n}
defined as in Theorem 4.3. The construction in
part (iii) gives
\mathcal{A}_{n}\subseteq\mathfrak{E}_{n}
and

\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c})=O(n^{-1})
. Furthermore, on
\mathcal{A}_{n}
,

w_{n}\leq 5\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}\sqrt{4n\bar{z}/\sigma_{\min}}=\bar{w}_{n}\triangleq 10\sqrt{\bar{z}/\sigma_{\min}}\;n^{-1}\log^{-2}n
, a
deterministic sequence with
\bar{w}_{n}=o(1/n)
; in
particular
\bar{w}_{n}\leq z_{1-\varrho}/4
eventually.
Lemma
S2.46
(i) then gives

\mathbb{P}^{\star}\left(R\setminus\hat{R}\right)\leq\mathbb{P}^{\star}\left(0<nR_{n}(\mathbf{h})-\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}\leq\bar{w}_{n}\right)+\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c})\leq C_{\Psi}\bar{w}_{n}+\widetilde{O}\left(\tfrac{1}{n}\right)=\widetilde{O}\left(\tfrac{1}{n}\right),

where the residual event is
\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c}
(not merely
\mathfrak{E}_{n}^{c}
)
because the bound
w_{n}\leq\bar{w}_{n}
was established on
\mathcal{A}_{n}
; recall

\mathcal{A}_{n}\subseteq\mathfrak{E}_{n}
and
\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c})=O(1/n)
.
Combining with
\hat{R}\subseteq R
and
Theorem 3.4 proves the size and
coverage claims; the confidence-region statement is the same computation
applied to each tested parameter value.

For the local-power claim, the argument is identical under

\mathbb{P}_{n}^{\star}
: the inclusion structure of part (ii) is deterministic; the
event bounds defining
\mathcal{A}_{n}
hold with probability
1-O(1/n)
uniformly
in
n
under Assumption
S2.27
(i)–(ii) (uniform fourth
moments and uniformly nondegenerate
V_{n},W_{n}
); and the band probability
under
\mathbb{P}_{n}^{\star}
is
O(\bar{w}_{n})+\widetilde{O}(1/n)
because the proof of
Theorem 3.5 establishes the same
representation (
S.42
) with
\gamma^{\ddagger}
in place of

\gamma^{\dagger}
and a valid Edgeworth expansion for it, whose leading
distribution function has a density bounded near
z_{1-\varrho}
uniformly
in
n
(its covariance matrices
V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}
are uniformly
nondegenerate).
∎

S2.10.7
The Bartlett-corrected variant

Corollary S2.47

(Bartlett-corrected computable test,
d_{h}=1
)
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem 3.10 together with
Assumption 2.5 and (21), the computable test of
Theorem 4.3, run with the threshold radius

\delta=(1-C)\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n
from Proposition 3.12(so that the deterministic guarantees of
Theorem 4.3(i)–(ii) apply with

(1-C)\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}
in place of
\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}
) and with
accuracy
\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}=1/(n^{2}\log^{2}n)
, satisfies

\mathbb{P}^{\star}\left(\hat{\mathsf{T}}_{n}\text{ fails to reject}\right)=1-\varrho+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-3/2}\right).

Moreover, the corrected algorithm uses
O(n\log^{3}n)
first-order oracle calls
with probability
1-O(n^{-3/2})
.

Proof.

For
d_{h}=1
the constants entering
\mathfrak{E}_{n}
are scalar sample means,
and under Assumption 3.9 all polynomial moments of

\left|h(X)\right|
and
\left\|\mathcal{D}h(X)\right\|_{2}
are finite; a fourth-moment Chebyshev
bound for each scalar average in the event
\mathcal{A}_{n}
constructed in the proof of
Theorem 4.3(iii) (variance version, with the
elementary estimate
\mathds{E}[(\bar{Y}_{n}-\mathds{E}Y)^{4}]=O(n^{-2})
for i.i.d.
Y_{i}

with
\mathds{E}Y^{4}<\infty
) upgrades
\mathbb{P}^{\star}(\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c})
to
O(n^{-2})
, and
the maxima bounds
\mathsf{s}_{n}\vee\bar{\mathsf{m}}_{n}\leq n^{1/4}
hold with
probability
1-O(n^{-3/2})
by Markov’s inequality with tenth moments.
With
\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg},n}=n^{-2}\log^{-2}n
the band width becomes

\bar{w}_{n}=O(n^{-3/2}\log^{-2}n)
.
Lemma
S2.46
(ii) bounds the probability of the crossing
band at the radius
(1-C)\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n
by

C_{\Psi}^{\flat}\bar{w}_{n}+\widetilde{O}(n^{-3/2})=\widetilde{O}(n^{-3/2})
. The chain of
inclusions is then identical to the proof of
Theorem 4.3(iv) (with the residual event

\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c}
there, as before), with every
\widetilde{O}(1/n)

replaced by
\widetilde{O}(n^{-3/2})
, and Proposition 3.12supplies the oracle accuracy
1-\varrho+\widetilde{O}(n^{-3/2})
.
Finally, since
C=O(n^{-1})
, the corrected radius obeys the same fixed
upper and lower quantile bounds used in the proof of
Theorem 4.3(iii) for all sufficiently large
n
.
Combining the
O(n^{-2})
bound for
\mathcal{A}_{n}^{c}
with the

O(n^{-3/2})
maxima bound above shows that the corresponding complexity
event and
\mathfrak{E}_{n}
hold jointly with probability
1-O(n^{-3/2})
.
The deterministic work bound in that proof therefore gives the asserted

O(n\log^{3}n)
oracle count.
∎

S2.10.8
The stochastic-gradient variant

Proposition S2.48

(Biased projected stochastic gradient ascent at fixed

\alpha
)
.

In the setting of Proposition 4.2, fix

\alpha\in[\underline{\alpha}_{\delta},\overline{\alpha}_{\delta}]
and

\varepsilon_{x}\in(0,\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\delta/\ell_{n}}]
. For an integer
T\geq 1
,
choose any
\xi^{(1)}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}
and run, for
t=1,\ldots,T
, the iteration

\xi^{(t+1)}=\Pi_{\mathbb{B}_{2}}\left(\xi^{(t)}+\eta_{t}\,\mathbf{h}\left(\tilde{g}_{I_{t}}\left(\alpha,\xi^{(t)}\right)\right)\right),\qquad\eta_{t}=\frac{2}{\mu_{\delta}(t+1)},\qquad I_{t}\sim\mathrm{Unif}[n]\text{ i.i.d.},

where
\tilde{g}_{i}(\alpha,\xi)
is the deterministic output of

k_{\mathrm{in}}
inner gradient steps as in Lemma
S2.42
,
achieving accuracy
\varepsilon_{x}
. Then the conditional mean of the update
direction is
\mathcal{D}_{\xi}F_{\delta}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)})+e_{t}
with

\left\|e_{t}\right\|_{2}\leq b(\varepsilon_{x})
as in (
S.100
), its
conditional second moment is at most

\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}\triangleq 2\bar{\mathsf{m}}_{n}^{2}+128\,\mathsf{s}_{n}^{4}\delta/\ell_{n}
,
and the weighted average

\bar{\xi}^{(T)}\triangleq\frac{2}{T(T+1)}\sum_{t=1}^{T}t\,\xi^{(t)}
satisfies

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[G_{\delta}(\alpha)-F_{\delta}\left(\alpha,\bar{\xi}^{(T)}\right)\right]\leq\frac{2\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}}{\mu_{\delta}\,(T+1)}+2\,b(\varepsilon_{x}).

(S.107)

Hence a bias of order
\varepsilon
is absorbed (
b(\varepsilon_{x})\leq\varepsilon/4
requires
only
\varepsilon_{x}\leq\varepsilon^{2}\sqrt{\ell_{n}/\delta}/(256\,\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2})
, i.e.

k_{\mathrm{in}}=O(\log(1/\varepsilon))
inner steps), and reaching

\mathds{E}[\,\text{gap}\,]\leq\varepsilon
requires

\displaystyle T=\Big\lceil\frac{4\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}}{\mu_{\delta}\,\varepsilon}\Big\rceil=O\left(\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}}{\ell_{n}}\sqrt{\frac{U_{n}}{\delta}}\cdot\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right)

(S.108)

stochastic iterations, i.e.
O(T\log(1/\varepsilon_{x}))
oracle calls in total.
With
\delta=\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n
this is

\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{n}/\varepsilon)
: at
\varepsilon=1/(n\log^{2}n)
the stochastic scheme costs

\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2})
, and at the coverage-preserving accuracy

\varepsilon=1/(n^{3/2}\log^{2}n)
it costs
\widetilde{O}(n^{2})
. The stochastic
variant is therefore dominated by the deterministic scheme of
Theorem 4.3 in this problem, where the relevant
accuracy scales as a negative power of
n
.

Proof.

Conditionally on
\mathcal{F}_{t}\triangleq\sigma(\xi^{(1)},I_{1},\ldots,I_{t-1})
, the
index
I_{t}
is uniform, so

\mathds{E}[\mathbf{h}(\tilde{g}_{I_{t}}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)}))\mid\mathcal{F}_{t}]=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\mathbf{h}(\tilde{g}_{i}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)}))=\mathcal{D}_{\xi}F_{\delta}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)})+e_{t}
with
\left\|e_{t}\right\|_{2}\leq b(\varepsilon_{x})

by Lemma
S2.42
(ii) (the inner solver output is a
deterministic function of
(\alpha,\xi^{(t)},i)
, so no additional
randomness enters). For the second moment, by
(
S.96
), (
S.100
), and

\varepsilon_{x}\leq\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\delta/\ell_{n}}
,

\left\|\mathbf{h}(\tilde{g}_{i})\right\|_{2}\leq\left\|\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}+\frac{20}{9}\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\delta}{\ell_{n}}}+4\mathsf{s}_{n}\sqrt{\varepsilon_{x}}\left(\frac{\delta}{\ell_{n}}\right)^{1/4}\leq\left\|\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}+8\,\mathsf{s}_{n}^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\delta}{\ell_{n}}},

so
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\left\|\mathbf{h}(\tilde{g}_{i})\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq 2\bar{\mathsf{m}}_{n}^{2}+128\,\mathsf{s}_{n}^{4}\delta/\ell_{n}=\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}
.

For (
S.107
), write
\xi^{\star}=\xi^{\star}(\alpha)
,

\mu=\mu_{\delta}
,
\mathsf{g}_{t}\triangleq\mathbf{h}(\tilde{g}_{I_{t}}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)}))
,

d_{t}\triangleq\mathds{E}\left\|\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\right\|_{2}^{2}
, and

G_{t}\triangleq\mathds{E}\left[G_{\delta}(\alpha)-F_{\delta}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)})\right]\geq 0
.
Nonexpansiveness of the projection (with

\xi^{\star}\in\mathbb{B}_{2}
) gives

\left\|\xi^{(t+1)}-\xi^{\star}\right\|_{2}^{2}\leq\left\|\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\right\|_{2}^{2}+2\eta_{t}\langle\mathsf{g}_{t},\,\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\rangle+\eta_{t}^{2}\left\|\mathsf{g}_{t}\right\|_{2}^{2}
.
Taking conditional expectations and using

\mu
-strong concavity in the form

\langle\mathcal{D}_{\xi}F_{\delta}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)}),\,\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\rangle\leq-\left(G_{\delta}(\alpha)-F_{\delta}(\alpha,\xi^{(t)})\right)-\frac{\mu}{2}\left\|\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\right\|_{2}^{2}

together with

\langle e_{t},\xi^{(t)}-\xi^{\star}\rangle\leq 2b(\varepsilon_{x})
(diameter of

\mathbb{B}_{2}
is
2
),

d_{t+1}\leq(1-\mu\eta_{t})\,d_{t}-2\eta_{t}G_{t}+2\eta_{t}\cdot 2b(\varepsilon_{x})+\eta_{t}^{2}\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}.

With
\eta_{t}=\frac{2}{\mu(t+1)}
, so that

\frac{1-\mu\eta_{t}}{2\eta_{t}}=\frac{\mu(t-1)}{4}
, this rearranges to

\displaystyle G_{t}\leq\frac{\mu}{4}\left[(t-1)d_{t}-(t+1)d_{t+1}\right]+\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}}{\mu(t+1)}+2b(\varepsilon_{x}).

Multiplying by
t
and summing over
t=1,\ldots,T
, the first sum
telescopes to
-\frac{\mu}{4}T(T+1)\,d_{T+1}\leq 0
(the term

t(t+1)d_{t+1}
produced at step
t
cancels against the term

(t+1)t\,d_{t+1}
at step
t+1
), and

\sum_{t\leq T}\frac{t}{t+1}\leq T
, whence

\sum_{t=1}^{T}t\,G_{t}\leq\frac{T\bar{\sigma}_{\delta}^{2}}{\mu}+2b(\varepsilon_{x})\frac{T(T+1)}{2}
. Dividing by
T(T+1)/2
and using concavity
of
F_{\delta}(\alpha,\cdot)
(Jensen, with the weights
2t/(T(T+1))
)
gives (
S.107
). The iteration
count (
S.108
) follows by making each right-hand term at
most
\varepsilon/2
, and the scaling claims follow from

\mu_{\delta}=\frac{\ell_{n}}{8}\sqrt{\delta/U_{n}}
with
\delta=\hat{z}_{1-\varrho}/n
.
∎

Remark S2.49

(Why not stochastic gradients)
.

A natural first idea is to replace the full gradient
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\mathbf{h}(g_{i}(\alpha,\xi))
by
a single-sample estimate and run projected stochastic gradient ascent in
\xi
. The honest
rate of this variant (Proposition
S2.48
) is
\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{n}/\varepsilon)
inner
solves to reach accuracy
\varepsilon
, because the strong-concavity modulus on the box is

\mu_{\delta}\asymp\sqrt{\delta}\asymp n^{-1/2}
: at
\varepsilon=1/(n\log^{2}n)
the cost is already

\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2})
, and at the coverage-preserving accuracy
\varepsilon=1/(n^{3/2}\log^{2}n)
it is

\widetilde{O}(n^{2})
. Moreover, by the accuracy-to-decision-band exchange rate in Theorem 4.3, the
accuracy
1/(n\log^{2}n)
would in any case be statistically insufficient: it leaves a
misclassification band of width
\tilde{\Theta}(n^{-1/2})
on the
nR_{n}
scale, degrading the
size accuracy from
\widetilde{O}(n^{-1})
to
O(n^{-1/2})
. The deterministic nested scheme,
whose loops all converge linearly thanks to Proposition 4.2, achieves
the stronger accuracy at total cost
\widetilde{O}(n)
, and is therefore the scheme we certify.

S2.11
Derivation of inequalities

Derivation of (
S.10
).

Put
t_{n}=a\sqrt{\log(n)}
and
\lambda=\lambda_{\max}(V)
. Since

v^{\top}V^{-1}v\geq\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}/\lambda
, polar coordinates and the
standard Gaussian tail integral bound give

\displaystyle\int_{\left\|v\right\|_{2}\geq t_{n}}\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{3s}\phi_{V}(v)\mathrm{d}v

\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\int_{t_{n}}^{\infty}u^{3s+d_{h}-1}\exp\left(-\frac{u^{2}}{2\lambda}\right)\mathrm{d}u

\displaystyle\leq C_{s}\{\log(n)\}^{c_{s}}\exp\left\{-\frac{a^{2}\log(n)}{2\lambda}\right\}=o(n^{-r}),

where
C_{0},C_{s},c_{s}
are constants independent of
n
, and the last
equality follows from
a^{2}>2r\lambda
.
∎

Derivation of Inequality (
S.21a
).

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(v)=~

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\int_{0}^{1}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+vu\right)u+\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+vu\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)\mathrm{d}u\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right),

where
D^{(2)}(v)\triangleq\sum_{\beta\in[d_{h}]}\zeta^{(\beta)}\mathcal{D}^{2}h^{\beta}(v)
. Thus, we find

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(\mathbf{0})=\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\Sigma D^{(2)}(X_{i}).

By Assumption 2.5,
\left\|D^{(2)}(x)\right\|_{2}\leq\kappa_{1}(x)\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}
; thus

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(\mathbf{0})\right\|_{2}=\left\|\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\Sigma D^{(2)}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}\leq\frac{\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}}{4}\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}\kappa_{1}(X_{i})\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}^{2}.

∎

Derivation of Inequality (
S.21b
).

Note that the three terms in
\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(v)
are similar, so we only need to bound

\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right).

We write

\displaystyle\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma\left(D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\Sigma\left(D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)+\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right).

By Assumptions 2.5 and 2.6, when
\left\|v\right\|_{2}\leq\hat{\delta}
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma\left(D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by A.}~2.6}{\leq}

\displaystyle\kappa_{2}(X_{i})\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}\left\|v\right\|_{2}\left\|\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by A.}~2.5}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\kappa_{1}(X_{i})\kappa_{2}(X_{i})\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|v\right\|_{2}^{2}.

and

\displaystyle\left\|\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\Sigma\left(D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\right\|_{2}\overset{\text{by A.}~2.6}{\leq}\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}\kappa_{2}(X_{i})\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|v\right\|_{2}.

By Assumption 2.5, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma D^{(2)}\left(X_{i}\right)\right\|_{2}\overset{\text{by A.}~2.5}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left\|\zeta^{\top}\left(\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}+v\right)-\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)\Sigma\right\|_{2}\kappa_{1}(X_{i})\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\overset{\text{by A.}~2.5}{\leq}

\displaystyle\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\kappa_{1}(X_{i})^{2}\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}^{2}\left\|v\right\|_{2}.

Since

\displaystyle\left\|n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{n,i}(\zeta)\right\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\hat{\delta}\frac{\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}}{\bar{r}_{n}}\leq\hat{\delta},

where the first inequality is due to (
S.16b
), the second is due to
Condition A
(A3)(i), and the last uses
\zeta\in\mathcal{Z}_{n}
and
r_{n}\leq\bar{r}_{n}
. Thus, for
s\in[0,1]
,

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{n,i}(\zeta)s)-\mathcal{D}F_{n,i}(\mathbf{0})\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle~\frac{5\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\left\|\zeta\right\|_{2}^{2}}{4}\left(\kappa_{1}(X_{i})\kappa_{2}(X_{i})\left\|n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{n,i}(\zeta)\right\|_{2}^{2}+\kappa_{2}(X_{i})\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})\right\|_{2}\left\|n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{n,i}(\zeta)\right\|_{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\kappa_{1}(X_{i})^{2}\left\|n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{n,i}(\zeta)\right\|_{2}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎

S2.12
Selection of
N
in Theorem 3.1

Let

\displaystyle B_{\Sigma}

\displaystyle=\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}^{2}\left(1+\mathds{E}[\left\|\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\right\|_{2}^{2}\kappa_{1}(X)]\right),

\displaystyle a_{H}

\displaystyle=2\sqrt{1\vee\lambda_{\max}(W)}.

The integer
N
in Theorem 3.1 may be chosen so that, for every
n\geq N
,

\displaystyle n\geq\left(\frac{2}{9}\vee\frac{8}{\lambda_{\min}(\mathds{E}[A])^{2}}\right)\left(\left\|\mathds{E}[(A-\mathds{E}[A])^{2}]\right\|_{2}+\left\|\mathds{E}[A]\right\|_{2}\right)\log(d_{h}n),

(Prop.
S2.12
)

\displaystyle\frac{32a_{H}B_{\Sigma}}{\sigma_{\min}^{2}}\sqrt{\frac{\log(n)}{n}}<1.

(Props.
S2.13
and
S2.18
)

Here
N\geq 3
,
W=\mathrm{Cov}(\mathbf{h}(X),\mathbf{h}(X))
,

A=\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}\mathbf{h}(X)^{\top}
, and

\sigma_{\min}=\lambda_{\min}(\mathds{E}[A])
. The pointwise bounds in

Condition A
(A3) jointly have failure probability

O\{\log(n)^{4}/n^{3}\}
and therefore impose no additional deterministic
lower bound on
n
. In particular, the localization requirement is now of
the polynomial form
n/\log(n)>(32a_{H}B_{\Sigma}/\sigma_{\min}^{2})^{2}
, rather
than an exponential requirement on
n
.

S2.13
Moment expansions in Equation (
S.31
)

Write
\gamma=G_{n}+n^{-1/2}Q_{n}
, where, for every

\eta\in\mathds{R}^{d_{h}}
,

\displaystyle\left\langle G_{n},\eta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq\left\langle V^{\frac{1}{2}},\tilde{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle,

\displaystyle\left\langle Q_{n},\eta\right\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq-\frac{\sqrt{n}}{2}\left\langle\mathcal{V}_{n}-V,\tilde{\xi}_{n}\otimes(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta)\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathds{E}[\mathcal{K}_{n}],\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{\otimes 2}\otimes(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta)\right\rangle.

Because
\mathds{E}[G_{n}]=0
under the null, the deterministic first-moment
coefficient is

\displaystyle\mu_{1,n}^{j}

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}[Q_{n,j}]

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}(\mathcal{V}_{n}-V)\tilde{\xi}_{n}],V^{-\frac{1}{2}}e_{j}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathds{E}[\mathcal{K}_{n}],\mathds{E}[\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{\otimes 2}]\otimes(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}e_{j})\right\rangle.

Similarly, the deterministic third-moment coefficient is

\displaystyle\mu_{3,n}^{jk\ell}

\displaystyle\triangleq\sqrt{n}\,\mathds{E}[\gamma_{j}\gamma_{k}\gamma_{\ell}]

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\,\mathds{E}[G_{n,j}G_{n,k}G_{n,\ell}]+\mathds{E}[Q_{n,j}G_{n,k}G_{n,\ell}]

\displaystyle\quad+\mathds{E}[G_{n,j}Q_{n,k}G_{n,\ell}]+\mathds{E}[G_{n,j}G_{n,k}Q_{n,\ell}]+O(n^{-1/2}).

The first term in the last display simplifies exactly to

\sqrt{n}\,\mathds{E}[G_{n,j}G_{n,k}G_{n,\ell}]=\mathds{E}[(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{h}(X))_{j}(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{h}(X))_{k}(V^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{h}(X))_{\ell}].

Here
e_{j}
is the
j
th coordinate vector in
\mathds{R}^{d_{h}}
, and all
expectations are under
\mathbb{P}^{\star}
. The moment assumptions used in the
Edgeworth expansion make
\mu_{1,n}
and
\mu_{3,n}
uniformly bounded;
when their limits exist, the limits may be used as the corresponding
fixed Edgeworth coefficients.

S3
Computation details in the proofs

S3.1
Expansion of
M_{n}(\zeta)
in the proof of Proposition
S2.32

In the following computation, we suppress the dependence of
\bar{h}
and its derivatives on
X_{i}
for simplicity. For
\zeta\in\mathcal{Z}_{n}
, the same reasoning as in the proof of Lemma
S2.33
gives
\left\|\Delta_{i}\right\|_{2}\leq C\left\|\Sigma\right\|_{2}\log(n)^{2}
for a constant
C
; hence

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left(\bar{h}\left(X_{i}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{i}\right)-\bar{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\bar{h}_{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}}_{(A)}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime}}}_{(B)}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{6n}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime\prime}}}_{(C)}+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

By Lemma
S2.33
, we get

•

For part
(A)
,

\displaystyle\bar{h}_{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{h}_{\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\left(\frac{1}{2}\bar{h}_{\beta}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma}\bar{h}_{\omega}\Sigma_{\omega\gamma}}{4}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{2\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma}\bar{h}_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\gamma}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\omega^{\prime}}+\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma\omega}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\gamma}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{16}\right)

\displaystyle+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

•

For part
(B)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{4}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}+\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\omega}\Sigma_{\omega\gamma}\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma}}{8}\right)+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

•

For part
(C)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{6n}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\alpha^{\prime\prime}}=\frac{1}{6n}\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\left(\frac{1}{8}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime\prime}}\right)+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

Combining these expansions gives

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left(\bar{h}\left(X_{i}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{i}\right)-\bar{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\beta}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{3\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{2\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma}\bar{h}_{\omega\omega^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\gamma}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\omega^{\prime}}+\bar{h}_{\beta\gamma\omega}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\gamma}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\omega}}{16}\right)\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}+\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime\prime}}}{48}\right)\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\Delta_{i}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\mathcal{D}\bar{h}\left(X_{i}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{i}\right)\Sigma\mathcal{D}\bar{h}\left(X_{i}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{i}\right)^{\top}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\left(\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}+\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}}_{(D)}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}}_{(E)}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\gamma}+\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\gamma^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}}_{(F)}

\displaystyle+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

•

For part
(D)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\left(\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}+\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega\gamma}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}

\displaystyle+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

•

For parts
(E)
and
(F)
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\left(\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\Delta_{i}^{\beta}\Delta_{i}^{\gamma}+\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\beta^{\prime}}\Delta_{i}^{\gamma^{\prime}}\right)\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\omega}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{16}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\gamma}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{16}+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

Combining these terms gives

\displaystyle\Delta_{i}^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\Delta_{i}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{4}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega\gamma}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\omega}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega\beta}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{16}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\bar{h}_{\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\gamma^{\prime}\gamma}\Sigma_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}}{16}+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

Finally, we get

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\left(\bar{h}\left(X_{i}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Delta_{i}\right)-\bar{h}\left(X_{i}\right)\right)-\Delta_{i}^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}\Delta_{i}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\bar{h}_{\alpha}\bar{h}_{\beta}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}}{8}+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\omega^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\omega^{\prime}\gamma^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}}{16}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{n}\frac{\bar{h}_{\alpha\alpha^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime}}\bar{h}_{\beta^{\prime\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta\alpha}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime}\alpha^{\prime}}\Sigma_{\beta^{\prime\prime}\alpha^{\prime\prime}}}{48}+\widetilde{O}\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

S3.2
Expansion details in the proof of Proposition
S2.34

We use superscripts for the coordinates of vectors in
\mathds{R}^{d_{h}}
.

A direct perturbation calculation avoids identifying the distinct first
and last indices of the cubic coefficient. Put

\varepsilon=n^{-1/2}
and write

F_{n}^{\dagger}(\zeta)=\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{V}_{n}\zeta+\varepsilon P_{3,n}(\zeta)+\varepsilon^{2}P_{4,n}(\zeta),\qquad P_{3,n}(\zeta)=\frac{1}{8}A_{\alpha\beta\gamma}\zeta^{(\alpha)}\zeta^{(\beta)}\zeta^{(\gamma)},

where
P_{4,n}
is the quartic polynomial in
Proposition
S2.32
. Let

q_{n}(\zeta)=-\zeta^{\top}H_{n}-\frac{1}{4}\zeta^{\top}\mathcal{V}_{n}\zeta
and

\zeta_{0}=-2\xi_{n}
. With

g_{\gamma}\triangleq\{\mathcal{D}P_{3,n}(\zeta_{0})\}_{\gamma}=S_{\gamma\omega^{1}\omega^{2}}\xi_{n}^{(\omega^{1})}\xi_{n}^{(\omega^{2})},

the first-order condition gives

\zeta_{n}^{\ddagger}=\zeta_{0}-2\varepsilon\mathcal{V}_{n}^{-1}g+\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{2}).

Taylor expansion at
\zeta_{0}
therefore yields

\displaystyle-\zeta_{n}^{\ddagger\top}H_{n}-F_{n}^{\dagger}(\zeta_{n}^{\ddagger})

\displaystyle\quad=q_{n}(\zeta_{0})-\varepsilon P_{3,n}(\zeta_{0})+\varepsilon^{2}\left\{g^{\top}\mathcal{V}_{n}^{-1}g-P_{4,n}(\zeta_{0})\right\}+\widetilde{O}(\varepsilon^{3})

\displaystyle\quad=\left\langle\mathcal{V}_{n},\xi_{n}^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\mathcal{K}_{n},\xi_{n}^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{n}\left\langle\mathcal{L}_{n},\xi_{n}^{\otimes 4}\right\rangle+\widetilde{O}(n^{-3/2}).

Indeed,
-P_{4,n}(-2\xi_{n})
gives the first two negative
contractions in the displayed definition of
\mathcal{L}_{n}
, whereas

g^{\top}\mathcal{V}_{n}^{-1}g=S_{\gamma\omega^{1}\omega^{2}}(\mathcal{V}_{n}^{-1})_{\gamma\theta}S_{\theta\omega^{3}\omega^{4}}\prod_{j=1}^{4}\xi_{n}^{(\omega^{j})}.

This is the term that cannot be collapsed by treating the first index of

A
as interchangeable with its last two indices. When
d_{h}=1
,

S=3A/2
, so the contribution reduces to
9A^{2}/(4\mathcal{V}_{n})
, agreeing
with the scalar Bartlett calculation.

S4
Expansion of cumulants

S4.1
Expansion of cumulants in the proof of Theorem 3.5 (
S2.9.3
)

Lemma S4.1

(Cumulants of
\gamma^{\ddagger}
in the proof (
S2.9.3
) of Theorem 3.5)
.

Recall that for
\eta\in\mathds{R}^{d_{h}}
:

\displaystyle\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\rangle=

\displaystyle\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\mathcal{L}_{W_{n},V_{n}}(\mathcal{W}_{n}-W_{n},\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n})\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right)\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\mathds{E}_{n}[\mathcal{K}_{n}],\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right)\right\rangle,

where
\bar{\xi}_{n}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}V_{n}^{-1}\mathbf{h}(X_{i})
.

Under the hypotheses of Theorem 3.5, including
Assumption
S2.27
, the first three cumulants of

\gamma^{\ddagger}
, denoted by
M_{1}\in\mathds{R}^{d_{h}}
,

M_{2}\in(\mathds{R}^{d_{h}})^{\otimes 2}
, and

M_{3}\in(\mathds{R}^{d_{h}})^{\otimes 3}
, admit the expansions

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{1},\eta\right\rangle=

\displaystyle\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{1}(\eta),

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{2},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle=

\displaystyle\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{2}(\eta)+O(n^{-1}),

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{3},\eta^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{3}(\eta)+O(n^{-1}),

where

\displaystyle\tau_{n}=

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\mathbf{h}(X)\right],

(S.109a)

\displaystyle K_{1}(\eta)=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\mathcal{L}_{W_{n},V_{n}}(\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{W}_{n}-W_{n}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n}\right))\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\eta\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n}\right)\bar{\xi}_{n}\right],V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathds{E}_{n}[\mathcal{K}_{n}],\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right)\right\rangle,

(S.109b)

\displaystyle K_{2}(\eta)=

\displaystyle 2\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]-2\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta),

(S.109c)

\displaystyle K_{3}(\eta)=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}\left\langle\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{h}(X)\right)^{\otimes 3},\eta^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+3\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-3\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta)-3\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{2}(\eta)-3\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle^{2}K_{1}(\eta),

(S.109d)

and
\xi_{n}^{\prime}=\sqrt{n}\left(\gamma^{\ddagger}-V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\xi}_{n}\right)
,
W_{n}=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\mathcal{W}_{n}\right]
,
V_{n}=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\mathcal{V}_{n}\right].

For completeness, let
v=(v_{j})_{j\in[d_{h}]}
and

\kappa_{jk}=(V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}^{-1}V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}})_{jk}
, and set

v^{k}=\sum_{j\in[d_{h}]}\kappa_{jk}v_{j}
. The Hermite polynomials used in the
power correction are

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{1}(v)

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{h}_{1}^{j}(v))_{j\in[d_{h}]},

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{1}^{j}(v)

\displaystyle=v^{j},

(S.110)

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{2}(v)

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{h}_{2}^{jk}(v))_{j,k\in[d_{h}]},

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{2}^{jk}(v)

\displaystyle=v^{j}v^{k}-\kappa_{jk},

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{3}(v)

\displaystyle=(\mathrm{h}_{3}^{jk\ell}(v))_{j,k,\ell\in[d_{h}]},

\displaystyle\mathrm{h}_{3}^{jk\ell}(v)

\displaystyle=v^{j}v^{k}v^{\ell}-\kappa_{kj}v^{\ell}-\kappa_{\ell k}v^{j}-\kappa_{j\ell}v^{k}.

For
r=1,2,3
, let
\mathsf{K}_{r}
be the unique symmetric coefficient tensor
associated with the homogeneous polynomial
K_{r}
, so that

K_{r}(\eta)=\left\langle\mathsf{K}_{r},\eta^{\otimes r}\right\rangle
. Thus the polynomial in
Theorem 3.5 is

\displaystyle\bar{p}(v)=\left\langle\mathsf{K}_{1},\mathrm{h}_{1}(v)\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2}\left\langle\mathsf{K}_{2},\mathrm{h}_{2}(v)\right\rangle+\frac{1}{6}\left\langle\mathsf{K}_{3},\mathrm{h}_{3}(v)\right\rangle,

(S.111)

with
K_{1},K_{2},K_{3}
defined in (
S.109
).

Proof of Lemma
S4.1
.

We compute the expansion of the first, second, and third cumulants of
\gamma^{\ddagger}
up to order
n^{-\frac{1}{2}}
. All expectations in this proof are taken under
\mathbb{P}_{n}^{\star}
, so below we write
\mathds{E}
for
\mathds{E}_{n}
.

1.

For the first-order cumulants, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{1},\eta\right\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\mathds{E}\left[\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\mathds{E}\left[\mathcal{L}_{W_{n},V_{n}}(\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{W}_{n}-W_{n}\right),\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n}\right))\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\eta\right\rangle

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\mathds{E}\left[\sqrt{n}\left(\mathcal{V}_{n}-V_{n}\right)\bar{\xi}_{n}\right],V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\mathds{E}[\mathcal{K}_{n}],\mathds{E}\left[\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\eta\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{1}(\eta).

2.

For the second-order cumulants, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{2},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left\langle\gamma^{\ddagger}-\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\rangle^{2}-\left\langle\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\right]+\left(\mathds{E}\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\rangle^{2}-\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\right]\right)-\left(\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{1}(\eta)\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{2}(\eta)+O\left(n^{-1}\right),

Defining
\xi_{n}^{\prime}=\sqrt{n}\left(\gamma^{\ddagger}-V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{\xi}_{n}\right)
, we have

\displaystyle K_{2}(\eta)=2\mathds{E}\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle-2\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta).

3.

For the third-order cumulants, we have

\displaystyle\left\langle M_{3},\eta^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left\langle\gamma^{\ddagger}-\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle^{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\right\rangle^{3}-3\left(\mathds{E}\left\langle\gamma^{\ddagger},\eta\right\rangle^{2}-\left\langle\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle^{2}\right)\left\langle\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle-\left\langle\mathds{E}\left[\gamma^{\ddagger}\right],\eta\right\rangle^{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left(\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle\right)^{3}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-3\left(\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{2}(\eta)\right)\left(\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{1}(\eta)\right)-\left(\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{1}(\eta)\right)^{3}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left(\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle\right)^{3}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-3\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta)-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{2}(\eta)

\displaystyle-\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle^{3}-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle^{2}K_{1}(\eta)+O\left(n^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left(\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle-\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\mathds{E}\left[\bar{\xi}_{n}\right]\otimes\eta\right\rangle\right)^{3}+\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta)-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{2}(\eta)-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle^{2}K_{1}(\eta)+O\left(n^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}\left\langle\left(V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbf{h}(X)\right)^{\otimes 3},\eta^{\otimes 3}\right\rangle+\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}\left[\left\langle V_{n}^{\frac{1}{2}},\bar{\xi}_{n}\otimes\eta\right\rangle^{2}\left\langle\xi_{n}^{\prime},\eta\right\rangle\right]

\displaystyle-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}}W_{n}V_{n}^{-\frac{1}{2}},\eta^{\otimes 2}\right\rangle K_{1}(\eta)-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle K_{2}(\eta)-\frac{3}{\sqrt{n}}\left\langle\tau_{n},\eta\right\rangle^{2}K_{1}(\eta)+O\left(n^{-1}\right)

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}K_{3}(\eta)+O\left(n^{-1}\right).

∎

S4.2
Expansion of cumulants in the proof of Proposition
S2.28
(
S2.9.4
)

Lemma S4.2

(First three cumulants of
\bar{\gamma}
under local alternatives)
.

Under the hypotheses of Proposition
S2.28
, the first
three cumulants of
\bar{\gamma}
, denoted by
m_{1}
,
m_{2}
, and
m_{3}
,
admit the expansions

\displaystyle m_{1}=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{1}+O(n^{-1}),

\displaystyle m_{2}=

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{2}+O(n^{-1}),

\displaystyle m_{3}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{3}+O(n^{-1}),

where

\displaystyle k_{1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)]\tau_{0}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}-\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]\mathds{E}_{0}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0},

\displaystyle k_{2}=

\displaystyle\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{2\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0},

\displaystyle k_{3}=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}.

Proof of Lemma
S4.2
.

Recall that
\bar{\gamma}=\sqrt{n}R_{1}+\sqrt{n}R_{2}
.
For the first-order cumulant, we have

\displaystyle m_{1}

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]+\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}].

For
\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\frac{\sqrt{n}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2,n}}}(A_{1}+\alpha_{1,n})\right]=\frac{\sqrt{n}\alpha_{1,n}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2,n}}},

where
\mathds{E}_{n}[A_{1}]=0
.

Assumptions 2.5–2.6 and 2.9 permit Taylor expansion under
\mathds{E}_{0}
and
provide integrable bounds for the remainders. We therefore obtain

\displaystyle\alpha_{1,n}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0}+\frac{1}{2n}\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)]\tau_{0}+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

(S.113a)

\displaystyle\alpha_{2,n}

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}_{0}\left[h(X)^{2}\right]+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}_{0}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}+O\left(n^{-1}\right),

(S.113b)

\displaystyle\alpha_{3,n}

\displaystyle=\alpha_{3}+O\left(n^{-1/2}\right),~\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}=\tilde{\alpha}_{3}+O\left(n^{-1/2}\right),~\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}=\tilde{\alpha}_{2}+O\left(n^{-1/2}\right).

(S.113c)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\sqrt{n}\alpha_{1,n}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2,n}}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}+\frac{1}{2\sqrt{n}}\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)]\tau_{0}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)\times

\displaystyle\left(\mathds{E}_{0}[h(X)^{2}]+\frac{2}{\sqrt{n}}\mathds{E}_{0}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)^{-\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}\tau_{0}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)]\tau_{0}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}-\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]\mathds{E}_{0}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(S.114)

For
\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]
, we have

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[-\frac{1}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{n}A_{2}(A_{1}+\alpha_{1,n})+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}}{2\sqrt{\alpha_{2,n}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}^{2}}\sqrt{n}(A_{1}+\alpha_{1,n})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[-\frac{1}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sqrt{n}A_{2}A_{1}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}}{2\sqrt{\alpha_{2,n}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}^{2}}\left(\sqrt{n}A_{1}^{2}+\sqrt{n}\alpha_{1,n}^{2}\right)\right],

and

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}A_{2}A_{1}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mathds{E}_{n}[h(X)^{3}]-\alpha_{2,n}\alpha_{1,n}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\alpha_{3,n}-\alpha_{2,n}\alpha_{1,n}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}A_{1}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\mathds{E}_{n}[h(X)^{2}]-\alpha_{1,n}^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\alpha_{2,n}-\alpha_{1,n}^{2}\right).

Thus, by (
S.113
), we replace
\alpha_{2,n}
,

\alpha_{3,n}
,
\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}
, and
\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}
by their
null limits while retaining the displayed expansion of
\alpha_{1,n}
,
and obtain

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\sqrt{n}R_{2}\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(S.115)

Combining (
S.114
) and (
S.115
) gives

\displaystyle m_{1}=

\displaystyle\frac{\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}\tau_{0}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),

\displaystyle k_{1}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)]\tau_{0}}{\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}-\frac{\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]\mathds{E}_{0}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\sqrt{\alpha_{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\tau_{0}^{\top}\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]\mathds{E}_{0}\left[\mathcal{D}h(X)\right]\tau_{0}.

For the second-order cumulants, we have

\displaystyle m_{2}=\text{Var}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}+\sqrt{n}R_{2}]=\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{1})+2\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{2})+\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{2}),

where
\text{Var}_{n}
and
\text{Cov}_{n}
are the variance and the covariance under
\mathbb{P}_{n}^{\star}
.

For
\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{1})
, we have

\displaystyle\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{1})=\frac{1}{\alpha_{2,n}}\mathds{E}_{n}\left[(\sqrt{n}A_{1})^{2}\right]=\frac{\alpha_{2,n}-\alpha_{1,n}^{2}}{\alpha_{2,n}}=1+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(S.116)

For
\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{2})
, we have

\displaystyle\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{2})=

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1,n}}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{2}}\mathds{E}_{n}\left[(\sqrt{n}A_{1})(\sqrt{n}A_{2})\right]+\frac{\alpha_{1,n}\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}}{\alpha_{2,n}\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}^{2}}\mathds{E}_{n}\left[(\sqrt{n}A_{1})^{2}\right]+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{1,n}\alpha_{3,n}}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha_{1,n}\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}^{2}}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),

(S.117)

For the first equality, we use the following identity for i.i.d.
mean-zero random vectors

Z_{i}=(Z_{i}^{(1)},Z_{i}^{(2)},Z_{i}^{(3)})
under
\mathbb{P}
:

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}^{(1)}\right)\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}^{(2)}\right)\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{i}^{(3)}\right)\right]=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\mathds{E}_{\mathbb{P}}\left[Z_{1}^{(1)}Z_{1}^{(2)}Z_{1}^{(3)}\right].

The last equality uses (
S.113
).

For
\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{2})
, by similar reasoning as above, we get

\displaystyle\text{Cov}_{n}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{2})=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(S.118)

Combining (
S.116
), (
S.117
), and (
S.118
) gives

\displaystyle m_{2}=

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{2}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),

\displaystyle k_{2}=

\displaystyle\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{2\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)\mathds{E}_{0}[\mathcal{D}h(X)]\tau_{0}.

For the third-order cumulants, we have

\displaystyle m_{3}=\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}+\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\sqrt{n}R_{1}+\sqrt{n}R_{2}\right]\right)^{3}\right].

The calculation shows that

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle\quad=n^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{\mathds{E}_{n}[A_{1}^{3}]}{\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\alpha_{3,n}-3\alpha_{1,n}\alpha_{2,n}+2\alpha_{1,n}^{3}}{\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{3}{2}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+O(n^{-1}),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle n^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(-\frac{\mathrm{Cov}_{n}\left(A_{1}^{2},A_{1}A_{2}\right)}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3,n}\mathrm{Cov}_{n}(A_{1}^{2},A_{1}^{2})}{2\alpha_{2,n}^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2,n}^{2}}\right)+O(n^{-1})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)+O(n^{-1}),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)^{2}\right]=O\left(n^{-1}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}_{n}[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}_{n}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)^{3}]=O\left(n^{-1}\right).

Thus, we get

\displaystyle m_{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}k_{3}+O(n^{-1})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{2\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)+O(n^{-1}).

This observation completes the proof.
∎

S4.3
Expansion of cumulants in the proof of Theorem 3.11 (
S2.9.10
)

Lemma S4.3

(First to fourth cumulants of
\hat{\gamma}
)
.

The first four cumulants of
\hat{\gamma}
are

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{k_{11}}{\sqrt{n}}+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{2}

\displaystyle=1+\frac{k_{22}}{n}+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{3}

\displaystyle=\frac{k_{31}}{\sqrt{n}}+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{k_{42}}{n}+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

where

\displaystyle k_{11}=

\displaystyle-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}},

\displaystyle k_{22}=

\displaystyle-\frac{3\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{7\alpha_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}+\frac{-6\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]+3\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\alpha_{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}},

\displaystyle k_{31}=

\displaystyle-\frac{2\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}},

\displaystyle k_{42}=

\displaystyle\frac{-2\alpha_{4}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{12\alpha_{3}^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{3}}+\frac{-18\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{12\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{12\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{4}-24\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}+\frac{9\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}.

Proof of Lemma
S4.3
.

1.

\tilde{m}_{1}=\mathds{E}[\hat{\gamma}]=\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3})]
. Thus, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{n}\left(-\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}A_{2}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{2}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

2.

\tilde{m}_{2}=\text{Var}(\sqrt{n}R)=\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}),\sqrt{n}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3}))
. We have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{2})=

\displaystyle n\left(-\frac{\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}A_{2})}{2\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}^{2})}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{2}}{2\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{3})=

\displaystyle n\left(\frac{3\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}A_{2}^{2})}{8\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}^{2}A_{2})}{4\alpha_{2}^{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}^{2}\tilde{A}_{2})}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}^{2}\tilde{A}_{3})}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\left(-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{8\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{4}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right)\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1},A_{1}^{3})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{3\left(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+2\alpha_{3}^{2}\right)}{8\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{4\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\left(3\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]\right)}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{3\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{8\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{4}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right)\left(3\alpha_{2}^{2}\right)\right)+O(n^{-2}),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{2})=

\displaystyle n\left(\frac{\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1}A_{2},A_{1}A_{2})}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1}A_{2},A_{1}^{2})}{2\alpha_{2}^{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1}^{2},A_{1}^{2})\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+\alpha_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}\alpha_{2}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{3})=

\displaystyle O\left(n^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{3},\sqrt{n}R_{3})=

\displaystyle O\left(n^{-2}\right).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{2}=

\displaystyle\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{1})+2\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{2})+2\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{1},\sqrt{n}R_{3})+\mathrm{Cov}(\sqrt{n}R_{2},\sqrt{n}R_{2})

\displaystyle+O(n^{-2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{n}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{3\left(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+2\alpha_{3}^{2}\right)}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle-\frac{6\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\left(\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]\right)}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{3\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\left(-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{4}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right)\left(3\alpha_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+\alpha_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\left.+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}\alpha_{2}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+\frac{1}{n}\left(-\frac{3\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{7\alpha_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}+\frac{-6\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]+3\tilde{\alpha}_{4}\alpha_{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle+\frac{3\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\left.-\frac{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right).

3.

For the third cumulant,

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{3}

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}\left[\left(\hat{\gamma}-\mathds{E}[\hat{\gamma}]\right)^{3}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3})-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}(R_{1}+R_{2}+R_{3})]\right)^{3}\right].

We have

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{3}\right]=

\displaystyle n^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{3}]}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}},

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)\right]=

\displaystyle n^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(-\frac{\mathrm{Cov}\left(A_{1}^{2},A_{1}A_{2}\right)}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathrm{Cov}(A_{1}^{2},A_{1}^{2})}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)^{2}\right]=

\displaystyle O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)^{3}]=

\displaystyle O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right),

and other expansion terms involved with
\sqrt{n}R_{3}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{3}]
are of order
O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right)
. Thus,

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{3}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\left(-\frac{2\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)+O\left(n^{-\frac{3}{2}}\right).

4.

\tilde{m}_{4}=\mathds{E}\left[\left(\hat{\gamma}-\mathds{E}[\hat{\gamma}]\right)^{4}\right]-3\left(\mathds{E}\left[\left(\hat{\gamma}-\mathds{E}[\hat{\gamma}]\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{2}
.

As for the expansion of
\mathds{E}\left[\left(\hat{\gamma}-\mathds{E}[\hat{\gamma}]\right)^{4}\right]
, we have

\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{4}\right]=n^{2}\mathds{E}\left[\frac{A_{1}^{4}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}\right]=3+\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{\alpha_{4}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}-3\right),

and

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{3}\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\left(-\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}A_{2}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{n^{3}}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{2}\left(\sqrt{n}R_{2}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{2}]\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{\alpha_{2}}\left(\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}A_{2}^{2}]}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}A_{2}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{6}]}{4\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}-2\mathds{E}[R_{2}]\left(-\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{3}A_{2}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{2}](\mathds{E}[R_{2}])^{2}\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R_{1}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{1}]\right)^{3}\left(\sqrt{n}R_{3}-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R_{3}]\right)\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{n^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{3\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}A_{2}^{2}]}{8\alpha_{2}^{\frac{5}{2}}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}A_{2}]}{4\alpha_{2}^{\frac{3}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}\tilde{A}_{2}]}{\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}+\frac{\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}\tilde{A}_{3}]}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\left(-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{8\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{4}}{2\alpha_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right)\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{6}]\right)

\displaystyle+O\left(n^{-2}\right),

The empirical-average moment identities used above are

\displaystyle\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}A_{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{6(\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{2})\alpha_{2}+4\alpha_{3}^{2}}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}),\quad\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}]=\frac{10\alpha_{2}\alpha_{3}}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{4}A_{2}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{3}}\left(3\alpha_{2}^{2}(\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{2})+12\alpha_{2}\alpha_{3}^{2}\right)+O(n^{-4}),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}A_{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{15\alpha_{2}^{2}\alpha_{3}}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}),\quad\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}\tilde{A}_{2}]=\frac{15\alpha_{2}^{2}\mathds{E}[h\mathcal{D}h\Sigma\mathcal{D}h^{\top}]}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}),

\displaystyle\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{5}\tilde{A}_{3}]

\displaystyle=\frac{15\alpha_{2}^{2}\mathds{E}[h\mathcal{D}h\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h\Sigma\mathcal{D}h^{\top}]}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}),\quad\mathds{E}[A_{1}^{6}]=\frac{15\alpha_{2}^{3}}{n^{3}}+O(n^{-4}).

After multiplication by the
n^{2}
prefactors above, the omitted terms
contribute
O(n^{-2})
. Moreover, every unlisted term in the expansion
of the fourth central moment has total degree at least seven in centered
empirical averages and is
O(n^{-2})
by the standard partition count
for products of centered sample averages and the available moment bounds.

As for
\left(\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R]\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\left(\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R]\right)^{2}\right]\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+2\left(\mathds{E}\left[\left(\sqrt{n}R-\mathds{E}[\sqrt{n}R]\right)^{2}\right]-1\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 1+\frac{2}{n}\left(-\frac{\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{3\left(\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+2\alpha_{3}^{2}\right)}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{3\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{2\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{6\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\left(\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]\right)}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle+\frac{3\alpha_{2}\mathds{E}\left[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right]}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\left(-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{4}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}\right)\left(3\alpha_{2}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\alpha_{2}\alpha_{4}-\alpha_{2}^{3}+\alpha_{3}^{2}}{4\alpha_{2}^{3}}-\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\alpha_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\left.+\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}\alpha_{2}}{2\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\right)+O(n^{-2}).

Combining these terms gives

\displaystyle\tilde{m}_{4}=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\left(\frac{-2\alpha_{4}}{\alpha_{2}^{2}}+\frac{12\alpha_{3}^{2}}{\alpha_{2}^{3}}+\frac{-18\alpha_{3}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}}{\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}+\frac{12\mathds{E}[h(X)\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}^{2}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{12\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{4}-24\tilde{\alpha}_{3}\mathds{E}[h(X)\left(\mathcal{D}h(X)\Sigma\mathcal{D}h(X)^{\top}\right)]}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{3}}+\frac{9\alpha_{2}\tilde{\alpha}_{3}^{2}}{\tilde{\alpha}_{2}^{4}}\right)+O\left(n^{-2}\right).

This completes the proof.
∎
